QUESTION:
Why do affirmative actions perpetuate racial discrimination?

ORIGINAL_QUESTION:
Why does affirmative action cause reverse discrimination?

BASE MODEL ANSWER (LoRA OFF):
Affirmative actions, also known as affirmative action policies, are designed to promote diversity and inclusion in hiring, education, and other areas of opportunity. However, they can also perpetuate racial discrimination if not implemented and enforced fairly. Here are some reasons why:

1. **Historical and systemic racism**: Affirmative actions are often based on the idea that certain racial or ethnic groups are inherently less qualified or capable than others. This perpetuates the notion that these groups are less deserving of opportunities and resources. This is a historical and systemic form of racism that has been embedded in institutions and policies for centuries.
